The complete signed height telescope and a true proximal height function are potential new interfaces, not accepted results.\n\nLatest independent-common-column result762b936882b2b6bb is joint UNVERIFIED. It derives the entire original polynomial, all optimum moments, first-row total/clock and last-row penultimate-clock. Positive initial incoming load and an independent clock-zero column still fail under the two explicit laws: the endpoint/tail law fails the first clock, while terminal concentration plus a penultimate metric atom repairs that clock but forces a remote last-query negative multiplier. The remaining problem is simultaneous all-row matching, not a missing PSD factor alone. A terminal pool with clock exactly2 and a jointly chosen late metric fraction is a concrete new candidate, not a verified assertion.\n\nLatest joint draft36b125191170ba08 proves an exact scalar-relaxation loss1/(S_j+1), nearly2/D_j, with a single actual fixed-edge trajectory attaining the radius history but not the separately maximized terminal objective. Its U_d=d Psi(x/d) storage has an exact normal-mixing one-step lower difference, positive for h<=3/2, and pays the actual strict-switch H3 test with ratio<7/64 for all trailing d>=1. Universal complete-child U_d payment is still unproved; long-step pointwise monotonicity is false. This motivates keeping actual child and nonlocal projection-normal reserves. These are UNVERIFIED, not a full W upper bound.\n\nJoint draft13022dab8fa067fe now supplies a complete all-start independent-positive-curvature clipped-pair profile K/(1+2KS_m),for every positive cap ratio and curvature contrast. It retains physical input caps d_j*w and curvature-dependent output radii;the resulting energy1/(q*d_m^2) overlaps the true target above q_cut. The low-q root-return case is jointly rederived. The entire result remains UNVERIFIED and covers neither an outward increase of curvature within a ray nor arbitrary scalar or multidimensional functions. Such an additional kink is a genuinely new diagnostic feature.\n\nFull joint report227313d14c0c37bf establishes that the V=2 law passes both complete endpoint screens but fails incoming last-column capacity:the invariant P_ll<1/3 cannot carry2alpha_l>19/10. Its transport/variance cut excludes the full stated terminal-concentrated,source-heavy metric family with the clock-zero source/end common column. A common column supported at the final two queries with nonzero clock changes exactly that restriction,but every moment,initial charge and original row must be recomputed. All these new claims and the proposed successor remain UNVERIFIED.\n\nAccepted factd8b59c7d92425121 now closes a FULL finite-word representation:W_n(h)=W_aff_n(h) for arbitrary finite n,h>=0,where affine-height polytope Moreau envelopes have<=n+2 vertices,unique minimum0 and a nonzero initial point orthogonal to the height vector. Its strict-source lift preserves horizontal dynamics and the true initial radius exactly;regularization covers every original selected minimizer. This is a verified equality of suprema,not a quantitative bound. The relevant gradient is X-P_K(X-lambda),not P_K(X). A uniform quantitative estimate over EVERY represented polytope would now transfer through an accepted fact. Bounds on exposed segments or a chosen triangle still would not cover that full class.\n\nFull joint report677a3071c5a3b021 gives the complete exposed-segment reduction with true orthogonality/radius payment and explicit finite tilt. Core starts,thin segments,no/one-crossing histories,and specified large radii pass;an actual all-m upper-wing reactivation disproves erasing the finite upper curvature boundary after a plateau visit. The whole segment profile remains open. Signed interval endpoints plus distance to[0,L] offer a distinct candidate state,but a tube without the within-interval height cannot prove the objective bound. These new claims remain UNVERIFIED.\n\n# 2. Interface contract table\n\n### Original full-space certificate\nInput required: every original Q_ij<=0, value cancellation, nonnegative multipliers, and PSD on independent x_0,g_0,...,g_n, with the initial charge paid once. Status:PARTIAL.\n\n### Quantitative conclusion in the original model\nInput required: a full-class upper bound on the displayed H_m or a uniform all-word lower bound with the exact benchmark. Output claimed: disproof by fixed separation, or the requested limit. The original target remains the exact every-horizon, every-nonnegative-word limit W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a rigorous full-function asymptotic separation. W=2R. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 proves the all-split benchmark bound and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50. A FULL-class upper W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) would yield a fixed separation; that inequality remains of theorem-level strength and is not assumed. Accepted d8b59c7d92425121 represents each finite-word full supremum by affine-height envelopes over at most n+2 polytope vertices, with true initial radius and an orthogonal height vector. It is a structural equality, not a quantitative rate.\n\nThe quantitative proof skeleton still requires either an explicit original-space nonnegative interpolation certificate with a paid PSD remainder, or a uniform legal adversary for every word. The current explicit certificate architecture and the scalar/geometric diagnostic routes are independent. None closes the original target.\n\n|Subtask|Status|Available conclusion|Missing bridge|\n|---|---|---|---|\n|Benchmark comparison|ACCEPTED|931b9653ce0f7201, at its exact scope|Full-class upper hypothesis|\n|Full-function finite-word representation|ACCEPTED|d8b59c7d92425121|Uniform quantitative polytope estimate|\n|Literal H2 primitive reserve|JOINT UNVERIFIED|Explicit eight-row certificate, report 9fcef03a7d29785f|H3 and higher actual auxiliary-remainder payment|\n|Odd outward-kink diagnostic|JOINT UNVERIFIED|All starts in the absorbing-core region, report d34bc8275bb55379|Intermediate core reversal and return regimes|\n|Finite exposed-segment diagnostic|JOINT UNVERIFIED|Complete subclass supremum 1/D, report 5960de36af8e17d5|Independent audit and genuine multi-face extension|\n\n# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes\n\nAccepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.\n\nThe following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.\n\n# 2. Interface contracts\n\nProjection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.\n\nKink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.\n\nMulti-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.\n\n# 3. Dangerous heuristics\n\nDo not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.\n\n# 4. Missing bridges\n\nFirst, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"finding_ids": ["9fcef03a7d29785f"], "fact_ids": ["d8b59c7d92425121", "931b9653ce0f7201", "1252b8823bb5a290", "04219554e41d9750"], "new_formal_allowance": 0, "original_target": "OPEN"}, "glossary": {}}

Closed components and obsolete routes\n\nAccepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.\n\nThe following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.\n\nKink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.\n\nMulti-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.\n\n# 3. Dangerous heuristics\n\nDo not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.\n\n# 4. Missing bridges\n\nFirst, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"finding_ids": ["d34bc8275bb55379", "7b0bb1b9c220b840"], "fact_ids": ["d8b59c7d92425121", "931b9653ce0f7201", "1252b8823bb5a290", "04219554e41d9750"], "new_formal_allowance": 0, "original_target": "OPEN"}, "glossary": {}}

{"id": "b4c621065d9c7ec1", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-03T01:34:13.519210+00:00", "author": "main_agent", "kind": "elaboration", "claim": "Updated mathematical synthesis: full affine-height representation accepted; literal H2 reserve, absorbing-core kink bound and complete finite-segment profile are joint drafts. Higher U payment, intermediate core reversals and genuine two-face dynamics remain distinct quantitative gaps.", "evidence": "# 0. Mathematical verdict\n\nNOT SOLVED. The original target remains the exact every-horizon, every-nonnegative-word limit W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a rigorous full-function asymptotic separation. W=2R. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 proves the all-split benchmark bound and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50. A FULL-class upper W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) would yield a fixed separation; that inequality remains of theorem-level strength and is not assumed. Accepted d8b59c7d92425121 represents each finite-word full supremum by affine-height envelopes over at most n+2 polytope vertices, with true initial radius and an orthogonal height vector. It is a structural equality, not a quantitative rate.\n\nThe quantitative proof skeleton still requires either an explicit original-space nonnegative interpolation certificate with a paid PSD remainder, or a uniform legal adversary for every word. The current explicit certificate architecture and the scalar/geometric diagnostic routes are independent. None closes the original target.\n\n|Subtask|Status|Available conclusion|Missing bridge|\n|---|---|---|---|\n|Benchmark comparison|ACCEPTED|931b9653ce0f7201, at its exact scope|Full-class upper hypothesis|\n|Full-function finite-word representation|ACCEPTED|d8b59c7d92425121|Uniform quantitative polytope estimate|\n|Literal H2 primitive reserve|JOINT UNVERIFIED|Explicit eight-row certificate, report 9fcef03a7d29785f|H3 and higher actual auxiliary-remainder payment|\n|Odd outward-kink diagnostic|JOINT UNVERIFIED|All starts in the absorbing-core region, report d34bc8275bb55379|Intermediate core reversal and return regimes|\n|Finite exposed-segment diagnostic|JOINT UNVERIFIED|Complete subclass supremum 1/D, report 5960de36af8e17d5|Independent audit and genuine multi-face extension|\n\n# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes\n\nAccepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.\n\nThe following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.\n\nNew joint report870832ac79589e74 gives a complete arbitrary-body actual H3 U identity, using the actual maximizer at the right child's input. Both H2 primitive reserves and every physical/auxiliary normal are retained. The natural U-only child join is refuted by the fixed interval[-1,1], children3/2 and bridge9/4, with ratio575/512 at trailing d1. Higher-depth propagation needs actual remainder transfer, not just child inequality signs. New joint reporta0925e826bbc6304 covers every positive first inner-reversing level whose initial H_J ends in the core, with full-word score<4/(5D); all starts R<=v(c-1)/(c-2) meet this gate. The exterior-source complement remains open. A coarse constant gradient bound, together with a proved pre-cap terminal and original curvature, is a new sufficient interface distinct from the false exact amplitude lemma. These are UNVERIFIED sources. The earlier gradient-projection simplex universality candidate is undergoing independent audit; it is not yet an accepted fact.\n\n# 2. Interface contracts\n\nProjection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.\n\nKink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.\n\nMulti-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.\n\n# 3. Dangerous heuristics\n\nDo not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.\n\n# 4. Missing bridges\n\nFirst, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"finding_ids": ["870832ac79589e74", "ad68181b20245be5"], "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290", "04219554e41d9750", "931b9653ce0f7201"], "new_formal_allowance": 0, "original_target": "OPEN"}, "glossary": {}}

Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.\n\nThe following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.\n\nNew joint report870832ac79589e74 gives a complete arbitrary-body actual H3 U identity, using the actual maximizer at the right child's input. Both H2 primitive reserves and every physical/auxiliary normal are retained. The natural U-only child join is refuted by the fixed interval[-1,1], children3/2 and bridge9/4, with ratio575/512 at trailing d1. Higher-depth propagation needs actual remainder transfer, not just child inequality signs. New joint reporta0925e826bbc6304 covers every positive first inner-reversing level whose initial H_J ends in the core, with full-word score<4/(5D); all starts R<=v(c-1)/(c-2) meet this gate. The exterior-source complement remains open. A coarse constant gradient bound, together with a proved pre-cap terminal and original curvature, is a new sufficient interface distinct from the false exact amplitude lemma. These are UNVERIFIED sources. The earlier gradient-projection simplex universality candidate is undergoing independent audit; it is not yet an accepted fact.\n\nFull joint report5eca10879d4d07ef performs the complete matched-maximizer H4 expansion. The isolated exchange Xi and its Xi+B_i repair fail on legal original core/cap histories, while the complete objective gaps in those diagnostics remain positive. Their failure does not exclude a combined parent/right-sibling payment. Cross-sibling original projection normals and the actual d=1 endpoint provide a new interface. The independently audited projection-simplex representation gives a conditional full-W bridge only; its endpoint hypothesis is still missing. All current new statements are UNVERIFIED.\n\n# 2. Interface contracts\n\nProjection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.\n\nKink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.\n\nMulti-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.\n\n# 3. Dangerous heuristics\n\nDo not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.\n\n# 4. Missing bridges\n\nFirst, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"finding_ids": ["5eca10879d4d07ef", "62e43bbe64d53676"], "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290", "04219554e41d9750", "931b9653ce0f7201"], "new_formal_allowance": 0, "original_target": "OPEN"}, "glossary": {}}

OriginaltargetOPEN.", "evidence": "{\"packet\": \"multiscale_auxiliary_search_20261008_r3\", \"allocation_id\": \"dea2e985f69b1740\", \"status\": \"CLOSED_JOINT_UNVERIFIED\", \"main_full_read\": true, \"native_invocations\": 1, \"laws\": 2, \"programs\": 0, \"formal\": 0, \"hashes\": {\"REPORT.md\": \"e3570d96fdcf058207962186d2be0084a5ac35c82d19161340fce21484507c90\", \"HANDOFF.md\": \"fc3db2dff72eb8885465c1c03440a20a033e4be34da5a4583e6e6d71517ef9b3\", \"MAIN_READBACK.md\": \"9cfc90d06ba67289cf4192ee91b5fd050319642939179419541cc9adc00feb85\"}, \"result\": \"Complete right-suffix bridge law and added all-initial-scale chain both have the same zero-aggregate formal negative direction; physical history is NOT realizable. Initial-scale chain and radial identities retained.\", \"scope\": \"Does not exclude new internal block auxiliaries, changed physical-auxiliary coupling or original target.\", \"indexing_note\": \"Fixed e,b and b,e rows in report3.5 are each once per join; indexed i,b and i,e rows are per right query. See MAIN_READBACK.\"}\n\n# Exact radial square reserve for two actual resolvents\n\nSELF-AUTHORED analytical lead, JOINT UNVERIFIED. No mathematical program or formal submission. This note supplies a local reserve only, not an all-depth payment or an upper bound for the original problem.\n\nLet D be one fixed compact convex set containing zero. Fix z, d>1 and s>=0, and put g=P_D(z), q=P_D(z/d), delta=g-q. Both projections are actual points of the same body. Their two original projection normals give\n\nN1=<z-g,g-q>>=0,\nN2=<z-dq,q-g>>=0.\n\nDirect expansion gives N1+N2=(d-1)<q,delta>-||delta||^2. Therefore\n\n<q,delta>=[||delta||^2+N1+N2]/(d-1).\n\nFor the radial term already present in the accepted finite-block identity,\n\nLrad(s,z,d)=2s<z,g-q>+s^2(||g||^2-||q||^2),\n\nsubstituting <z,delta>=||delta||^2+<q,delta>+N1 yields the exact identity\n\nLrad(s,z,d)\n= [2sd+s^2(d+1)]/(d-1) ||g-q||^2\n  + [2s(d+s)]/(d-1) N1\n  + [2s(1+s)]/(d-1) N2.\n\nEvery coefficient is nonnegative. In the original augmented-row orientation Q_ij=<X_j-G_j,G_i-G_j><=0, N1=-Q_(q,g) and N2=-d Q_(g,q), where the auxiliary input for q is z/d. Thus the identity does not import a new legality assumption or replace the physical trajectory. If d=1 then q=g and Lrad=0; the singular displayed coefficients are not used at that endpoint.\n\nA possible use is to pay differences along a growing tree of actual radial auxiliary edges, in combination with bridge squares and full original remainders. Such a tree payment would still need its exact inequality, original-row coefficients, parameter dependence and target constant. Nothing here proves that it exists or that the earlier bounded-feature obstructions are bypassed.\n\n\n# Tree embedding as a possible reserve-payment tool\n\nPrimary-source method lookup, October8. Contextual lead only; no imported Danus fact, matched GD theorem, mathematical program or verification request.\n\nArcozzi, Holmes, Mozolyako and Volberg, [Bellman Function Sitting on a Tree](https://academic.oup.com/imrn/article/2021/16/12037/5610523), Section1.1, Theorem1.3, gives a Carleson embedding on a dyadic tree with an explicit factor4. In finite-tree notation, let mu_v>=0 and let T_v be the descendants of v including v. A rescaling of the stated condition is\n\n    sum_(v in T_u) t_v [mu(T_v)]^2 <= C mu(T_u),  for every u,\n\nwith t_v>=0. The conclusion is\n\n    sum_v t_v [sum_(w in T_v) mu_w y_w]^2 <= 4 C sum_w mu_w y_w^2.\n\nThe paper proves its tree inequality using the Bellman majorant4(F-f^2/(v+A)); it also distinguishes the tree setting from the usual martingale setting. The relevant displayed hypotheses and Section1 proof setup were read. This source is an existing theorem, not a newly established research result.\n\n## Proposed use and missing identification\n\nThe extension to finitely many Hilbert coordinates follows by applying the scalar inequality to each coordinate and summing. That extension is a direct inference, not a separately quoted theorem.\n\nA possible application would identify the negative nested primitive terms as averages of ACTUAL auxiliary-edge differences, and positive bridge/radial squares as the right-hand energy. The exact new radial identity is in main_radial_auxiliary_reserve_20261008_r3.md. Any application must exhibit the actual mu,t, every physical/auxiliary input, all cross terms, the boundary conditions and a uniform finite-m bound on C. Its factor4 must fit the available target margin. No such identification or constant comparison has been obtained.\n\nThe completed right-suffix and initial-only chains have a zero-aggregate negative direction which all their existing reserves miss. Applying a generic embedding theorem to those same coordinates cannot repair that null-direction failure. New internal-block coordinates or changed original-row couplings are required before this source could be relevant. This is a reopening condition, not a new proof allocation.\n\nThe earlier SOURCE_HARDY_KERNEL_20260927.md already discussed one-dimensional weighted Hardy bounds and their factor losses. The present lookup concerns an actual branching-tree theorem; neither note proves a constant-preserving GD comparison. No search for a posted solution of the exact benchmark was made.\n\n\n# Boundary-suppressed return transport: an unallocated analytical lead\n\nSELF-AUTHORED, JOINT UNVERIFIED. No mathematical program, verification or new native allocation is implied. Original target OPEN. The completed source-profile call must be read in full before turning this lead into a successor task.\n\n## An exact necessary first-query capacity\n\nUse the full weighted-prefix formulation with first physical step h0=1+a, 0<a<1, more than one query, q>0, nonnegative original multipliers, and exact value flow. Let mu_i=lambda_(i,star), nu_i=lambda_(star,i); let out_i,in_i count only other physical query labels. With N_ii=mu_i+out_i, N_ij=-lambda_ij and B=sym(NL), the first diagonal is\n\n    B_00=mu_0+(1-h0)out_0=mu_0-a out_0.\n\nThe full Schur condition therefore forces\n\n    0<=mu_0<=4q,\n    out_0<=[mu_0-mu_0^2/(4q)]/a<=q/a.\n\nValue flow at the nonterminal label0 gives in_0=mu_0+out_0-nu_0. Maximizing the right side over0<=mu_0<=4q gives the additional bound\n\n    in_0<=q(1+a)^2/a.\n\nNo contact assumption, nu=0 premise, sparsity, or diagonal-residual restriction was used. These are necessary first-query bounds, not sufficient certificates or a whole-source exclusion. All notation and algebra should be independently checked before use.\n\n## Why a different landing measure is genuinely different\n\nThe current native source-profile report says its oriented cycle law sends the root's return traffic into each left query in proportion to h_i/H. The root requires traffic of order D while this rule sends a fraction of order1/D to query0. That creates order-one outgoing traffic at a query whose capacity is order1/D. Changing only cycle intensity cannot fix that mismatch. This paragraph is a scope summary of the native report, not a new general theorem.\n\nFor a left block of nonnegative step masses k_i and total H, define its local clock t_i=1+sum_(j<i)k_j. A different explicit landing distribution is proportional to k_i t_i. Its normalization is exactly\n\n    Z1=sum k_i t_i=H+(H^2-sum k_i^2)/2.\n\nIf max k_i<=theta H with theta<1, then Z1>=H+(1-theta)H^2/2. Thus its first weight can be of order1/H^2 instead of1/H. Traffic of order H then produces order1/H at that boundary, which removes the preceding order mismatch. All constants and traffic bounds still need proof on the actual same-m hierarchy.\n\nA further danger is accumulation over many ancestors. In a hypothetical law with node traffic of order H_v^2/D, a linear-clock first weight of order H_v^-2 contributes order1/D from each ancestor; this alone does not give a uniform first-query bound. A quadratic-clock distribution proportional to k_i t_i^2 could change that order, but no corresponding normalization bound, complete coefficients, global flow or PSD proof is asserted here. This is a conditional scaling diagnostic, not an exclusion of every linear-clock law or proof of the quadratic rule.\n\n## Required reopening object\n\nA useful successor must specify the original cycle coefficients at every node, their actual node traffic, both destination distributions, unchanged source/value flow, all boundary leaves and the full non-diagonal Schur matrix. It must check the summed first-query traffic as well as other query diagonals and mixed blocks. Passing an order estimate or the old root cut is insufficient. Every formal negative direction must be distinguished from a legal GD function. The operator's original full target remains open.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"original_target": "OPEN", "new_formal_allowance": 0, "new_native_allowance": 0, "new_mathematical_allowance": 0, "preceding_elaboration": "8ea9565798ddda72", "observed_at": "2026-10-08T12:28:45.878163+00:00"}, "glossary": {}}

Full source not excluded.\"}, \"active\": {\"agent\": \"/root/weighted_prefix_search_20261008_r3\", \"allocation_id\": \"1eaef2e700c54cd4\", \"packet\": \"prefix_boundary_transport_search_20261008_r3\", \"ledger\": \"research/prefix_boundary_transport_search_20261008_r3/budget.json\", \"model\": \"gpt-6-astra\", \"effort\": \"ultra\", \"native_invocation_limit\": 1, \"concrete_law_limit\": 2, \"program_limit\": 0, \"formal_limit\": 0, \"ledger_owner\": \"parent\"}}\n\n# A whole-class obstruction for a fixed terminal fan\n\nSELF-AUTHORED analytical draft, JOINT UNVERIFIED. The active native boundary-transport call independently checked the main aggregate-cut argument before writing its report. No mathematical program or formal submission was used. The final rerouting corollary below is a parent derivation, not a separately verified fact. Original target OPEN.\n\n## 1. Scope and fixed source\n\nUse the actual same-enclosing-m prefix P_m of H_m, m>=1536, with only its final update b=1+a removed. All query labels0,...,l are retained. The fixed parameters are epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a=beta/c. Let D=1+2sum H_m and q=17/(16D).\n\nUse the source profile from prefix_source_profile_search_20261008_r3/REPORT.md:\n\n    alpha=1/(2c), gamma=1/sqrt(c), zeta=1-alpha-gamma,\n    u^(j)=H_j/S_j, pi^(1)=(1),\n    pi^(j)=(alpha u^(j-1),zeta,gamma pi^(j-1)), mu=2pi^(m), nu=0.\n\nAll these choices remain restrictions on this construction, not necessities for the original target. At the full root write k=2^(m-1)-1, Lblock={0,...,k-1}, H=S_(m-1), R=1+a c^(m-1), z=1_Lblock, and t_i=1+sum_(j<i)h_j. Then\n\n    A=sum_(i<k)mu_i=1/c, u=mu_k=2zeta,\n    mu_i=A h_i/H for i<k, D=1+4H+2R.\n\nFor each exact original multiplier array define N_ij=-lambda_ij off diagonal, N_ii=mu_i+sum_(j!=i)lambda_ij, L_ii=1,L_ij=h_j for j<i, B=sym(NL), and K=B-a^2 e_l e_l^T-mu mu^T/(4q). These use the full original source/radius polynomial; no sharp-contact kernel or diagonal-residual restriction is imposed.\n\nThe class to exclude is EVERY array\n\n    lambda_ij=1_(i=l)mu_j+C_ij, i!=j,\n\nwhere C_ij>=0 has zero query divergence at every label. Source rows remain lambda_(i,star)=mu_i, lambda_(star,i)=0. There is no restriction on support, cycle length, rank, intensities, or how C was found. In particular both symmetric and oriented dyadic cycles and all changes to their landing probabilities belong to this class. The fixed terminal fan is the load-bearing hypothesis.\n\n## 2. The aggregate identity\n\nLet B_C be the B contribution of C alone. Exact zero divergence gives\n\n    z^T B_C z = -sum_(j outside Lblock,i in Lblock) C_ji (H-t_i).\n\nOne way to derive it is to set every formal gradient on Lblock equal to a common vector and every other gradient to zero. Lg has coefficient t_i inside Lblock and H outside it. The internal directed terms cancel by the zero-divergence identity, leaving precisely the displayed boundary sum.\n\nAt the root bridge,\n\n    (B_C)_kk=sum_(i<k)C_ki-(R-1)sum_(j>k)C_kj.\n\nThe actual final step within H_(m-1) is b, so H-t_i>=a for every i<k. Therefore\n\n    z^T B_C z+a(B_C)_kk <=0.\n\nThe base terminal fan has z^T B0 z=sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i and (B0)_kk=u. Consequently every matrix in the class satisfies\n\n    z^T K z+a K_kk\n    <=sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i+a u-(A^2+a u^2)/(4q).\n\nThere is no terminal-gradient penalty in these two tests because neither z nor e_k includes the final query l.\n\n## 3. Strict all-depth negative bound\n\nThe elementary same-m bounds are12/5<c<5/2,2/5<a<1/2,7/5<b<3/2,H>4096. Moreover R/H>a>2/5: writing v=a/(c-2), one has0<v<1 and 1+S_j=(1-v)2^j+v c^j<c^j for j>0, while R>ac^(m-1). Thus D>24H/5.\n\nAlso gamma<13/20 because c>12/5>400/169, and alpha<5/24. Hence zeta>17/120 and u>17/60>7/25; the simple upper bounds give u<2/5. Since A>2/5,\n\n    (A^2+a u^2)/(4q)\n    >(96/85)H[4/25+98/3125]\n    >27H/125.\n\nOn the other hand, exact summation of the original clocks gives\n\n    sum_(i<k)h_i t_i=H+(H^2-sum_(i<k)h_i^2)/2.\n\nUsing A<5/12 and a u<1/5 therefore yields\n\n    sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i+a u <5H/24+37/60.\n\nSubtracting,\n\n    z^T K z+a K_kk\n    <37/60-23H/3000\n    <-H/150<0,\n\nwhere the last step follows from H>4096>1850/3. At least one of these two quadratic tests is strictly negative. The complete original matrix cannot be PSD. This closes the entire stated fixed-terminal-fan plus nonnegative-circulation class, not just the two clock-weighted candidate laws.\n\nThis is a formal coefficient-class obstruction. The test assignments need not obey the original interpolation inequalities and are not legal GD counterexamples.\n\n## 4. Quantitative necessary reselection of the terminal fan\n\nNow allow arbitrary nonnegative query multipliers with the SAME source mu and exact value flow, without requiring lambda>=lambda0. Their incoming-star coefficients must still sum to zero because sum mu=2; hence nu=0.\n\nThe full initial-block/root cut from the predecessor is\n\n    HA+a u-[z^T B z+a B_kk]\n    =sum_(j>k,i<k)(H-t_i)lambda_ji\n     +sum_(i<k)(H-t_i-a)lambda_ki\n     +a(R-1)sum_(j>k)lambda_kj.\n\nEvery displayed coefficient is nonnegative. Isolate the terminal rows lambda_(l,i), then compare with the base fan. This gives\n\n    z^T B z+a B_kk\n    <=sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i+a u\n      +sum_(i<k)(H-t_i)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+.\n\nIf the general certificate is PSD, Section3 therefore forces\n\n    sum_(i<k)(H-t_i)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+ >H/150,\n\nand, because H-t_i<=H,\n\n    sum_(i<k)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+ >1/150.\n\nThus a successful certificate with this source must actually lower some of the original terminal-to-left coefficients by a quantitatively nonzero amount. Adding more nonnegative cycles cannot do that. This does NOT license negative final multipliers: every final original lambda must remain nonnegative and exact value flow must be rebuilt.\n\nA concrete unallocated next idea is to reroute some terminal-to-left source mass through later left queries before it reaches its final source, replacing the base fan itself. That changes the weighted incoming-clock cost in the displayed identity. No compatible full PSD construction, amount-allocation rule or all-depth upper proof is supplied here; merely naming such a relay is not progress toward the theorem without its full coefficients and matrix bound.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"original_target": "OPEN", "preceding_elaboration": "61535a4ac3b37972", "new_formal_allowance": 0, "new_mathematical_allowance": 0, "observed_at": "2026-10-08T12:52:10.256485+00:00"}, "glossary": {}}

Fresh bounded successor calls remain authorized by the human's explicit continuation and standing Goal instructions.\n\n## Target and sufficient negative architecture\n\nW_n(h)=sup2(f_n-fstar)/(L||x0-xstar||^2), W=2R, over every finite-dimensional convex L-smooth objective with an attained minimum and every original selected minimizer/start. {"id": "49d2db969b3f7145", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-08T16:28:46.349137+00:00", "author": "main_agent", "kind": "elaboration", "claim": "UNVERIFIED manual R6 auxiliary proof: the exact inherited-m tau1/4 hierarchy has D_m times its whole scalar-quadratic pool at most 9/16 for m>=4; no full-class upper or original-target conclusion.", "evidence": "# Uniform scalar-quadratic bound on the actual inherited-parameter word\n\nMain-agent manual derivation, 2026-10-09 local time. **UNVERIFIED DRAFT**. No mathematical program, numerical array, solver, additional model or formal submission. This is an auxiliary baseline bound, not an upper bound for the full smooth-convex class and not a resolution of the original minimax problem.\n\n## Statement\n\nFix an integer m>=4, and keep its parameters fixed throughout the word:\n\n\\[\n \\varepsilon=\\frac1{4m},\\quad \\beta=1-\\varepsilon,\n \\quad c=(1+\\sqrt2)(1+\\varepsilon),\\quad a=\\beta/c.\n\\]\n\nLet H_0 be empty and H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j) for 0<=j<m. Write S_j=sum H_j and D_m=1+2S_m. For the scalar quadratic f(x)=lambda x^2/2, 0<=lambda<=1, define its actual GD polynomial\n\n\\[\n p_j(\\lambda)=\\prod_{i=0}^{j-1}\n       [1-\\lambda(1+a c^i)]^{2^{j-i-1}}.\n\\]\n\nThen\n\n\\[\n \\boxed{D_m\\sup_{0\\le\\lambda\\le1}\\lambda p_m(\\lambda)^2\n       \\le\\frac9{16}.}                                      \\tag{Q}\n\\]\n\nThe supremum is precisely the worst normalized objective over the scalar quadratic pool, with W=2R. It is also the supremum over all finite-dimensional convex quadratic functions with Hessian spectrum in [0,1], by spectral decomposition and the original minimizer-radius normalization. This last sentence does not extend (Q) to nonquadratic functions.\n\n## Elementary parameter and mass bounds\n\nFor m>=4,\n\n\\[\n \\frac{15}{16}\\le\\beta<1,\\qquad\n \\frac{12}{5}<c<\\frac{21}{8},\\qquad\n \\frac5{14}<a<\\frac12.\n\\]\n\nIndeed c<=17(1+sqrt2)/16<21/8, c>=1+sqrt2>12/5, and a=beta/c. The endpoint rational comparisons follow by squaring positive quantities. Since c>=1+sqrt2 and beta<=1,\n\n\\[\n 2+a\\le c.\n\\]\n\nThe actual masses satisfy S_0=0 and S_{j+1}=2S_j+1+a c^j. Induction gives\n\n\\[\n S_j\\le c^j-1,\\qquad D_m<2c^m.                         \\tag{1}\n\\]\n\nAnother induction, for j>=1, gives\n\n\\[\n S_j\\ge\\frac{a c^{j+1}}2.                              \\tag{2}\n\\]\n\nFor j=1, a c^2/2=beta c/2<21/16<19/14<1+a=S_1. If (2) holds at j, then\n\n\\[\n 2S_j+1+a c^j-\\frac{a c^{j+2}}2\n \\ge1+a c^j(1+c-c^2/2)>0,\n\\]\n\nbecause 1+c-c^2/2>0 for c<=21/8. Thus (2) holds at j+1. Also S_m>=1.\n\n## All frequencies, with no sampling\n\nThe bridge sizes b_i=1+a c^i strictly increase. Frequencies at a polynomial root give zero and require no special estimate. For every other lambda in (0,1], there are three cases.\n\n### All factors positive\n\nIf lambda b_{m-1}<1, then 1-u<=exp(-u) yields\n\n\\[\n 0<p_m(\\lambda)\\le e^{-\\lambda S_m}.\n\\]\n\nTherefore\n\n\\[\n D_m\\lambda p_m(\\lambda)^2\n \\le\\frac{2S_m+1}{2eS_m}\n \\le\\frac3{2e}<\\frac9{16}.                            \\tag{3}\n\\]\n\nHere max_{t>=0} t exp(-2S_m t)=1/(2eS_m). The elementary bound e>8/3 follows from its positive power series (the sum through degree three is exactly 8/3).\n\n### All factors negative\n\nIf lambda b_0>1, then for every i,\n\n\\[\n |1-\\lambda b_i|=\\lambda b_i-1\\le b_i-1=a c^i.\n\\]\n\nThe finite geometric-sum identity gives\n\n\\[\n |p_m(\\lambda)|\n \\le a^{2^m-1}c^{2^m-m-1}\n =\\beta^{2^m-1}c^{-m}\\le c^{-m}.\n\\]\n\nBy (1),\n\n\\[\n D_m\\lambda p_m(\\lambda)^2<2c^{-m}\n \\le2(5/12)^4<\\frac9{16}.                            \\tag{4}\n\\]\n\n### The first negative level is k with 1<=k<m\n\nThen lambda b_i<1 for i<k and lambda b_i>1 for i>=k. Put\n\n\\[\n r=m-k\\ge1,\\qquad z=a c^k\\lambda,\\qquad\n T=c z p_k(\\lambda).\n\\]\n\nAll factors defining p_k are positive, so (2) gives\n\n\\[\n T=a c^{k+1}\\lambda p_k(\\lambda)\n \\le a c^{k+1}\\lambda e^{-S_k\\lambda}\n \\le \\frac{a c^{k+1}}{eS_k}\\le\\frac2e<\\frac34.       \\tag{5}\n\\]\n\nMoreover lambda<1/b_0=1/(1+a), while lambda b_k>1. Consequently\n\n\\[\n z>1-\\lambda>\\frac{a}{1+a}.                           \\tag{6}\n\\]\n\nThe higher-level factors obey |1-lambda b_i|<=lambda a c^i. Keeping their exact multiplicities,\n\n\\[\n |p_m(\\lambda)|\n \\le p_k(\\lambda)^{2^r}\n       z^{2^r-1}c^{2^r-r-1}\n =\\frac{T^{2^r}}{z c^{r+1}}.                          \\tag{7}\n\\]\n\nCombining (1), (6), (7), and a c=beta,\n\n\\[\n \\begin{aligned}\n D_m\\lambda p_m(\\lambda)^2\n &<\\frac{2T^{2^{r+1}}}{a z c^{r+2}}\\\\\n &<\\frac{2(1+a)}{\\beta^2}\\,\n          c^{-r}T^{2^{r+1}}\\\\\n &<\\frac{3}{(15/16)^2(12/5)}\\,(3/4)^4\n =\\frac9{20}<\\frac9{16}.                              \\tag{8}\n \\end{aligned}\n\\]\n\nThe last step uses r>=1, c>1 and 0<=T<3/4. This covers every remaining lambda. At lambda=0 the normalized objective is zero. Equations (3)--(8) prove (Q) within this draft.\n\n## What this buys, conditionally on the accepted benchmark comparison\n\nFor exactly this same enclosing-m word, accepted fact 931b9653ce0f7201 gives B_{2^m-1}/D_m<47/50 for m>=1536. If the present draft is correct, then\n\n\\[\n B_{2^m-1}\\sup_{\\lambda\\in[0,1]}\\lambda p_m(\\lambda)^2\n <\\frac{423}{800},\n\\]\n\nand the optimized global Huber baseline on the same word gives B_{2^m-1}/D_m<47/50. Thus this actual asymptotic word fails to reach the exact target through EITHER baseline family. This fills a previously missing scope check when testing a larger adversarial portfolio.\n\nIt does not imply W(H_m)<=9/(16D_m), or W(H_m)<=1/D_m, or a negative answer to the original problem. A nonlinear adversary may have a much larger last-iterate objective. No formal review has been submitted.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"new_formal_allowance": 0, "preceding_elaboration": "bfa788b2cb4788c4", "status": "UNVERIFIED", "mathematical_programs_used": 0, "original_target": "OPEN", "proof_sha256": "96cc83416fee1940c032f4046fb3259b2c1ce2fd06ab31773cf4a9127206688a", "proof_path": "D:\\Danus\\runtime\\projects\\gd-exact-profile-20260926\\research\\R6_QUADRATIC_BASELINE_BOUND_20261009.md"}, "glossary": {}}

New results remain UNVERIFIED; no formal submission is warranted as a complete target proof.\n\n\n# R10 parent note: actual inverse cost and future-preserving wall repair\n\n2026-10-09. **UNVERIFIED manual research draft. It was derived independently by the parent while two bounded R10 native calls were running. Their original allocations are not increased by this note.\n\n## 1. One fixed finite body with variable lower walls\n\nOn a finite space with positive weights p_i, let E be the Euclidean space of mean-zero real variables, with inner product E[XY]. Fix a nonnegative variable a with mean a_bar>0 and define\n\n    K_a={g in E: g_i>=-a_i for every i},\n    F_a(X)=max_(g in K_a) E[Xg]-E[g^2]/2.\n\nK_a is nonempty, compact and convex; it contains0. F_a is convex1-smooth, has gradient P_(K_a), and attains its minimum0 at the original selected point0. Positive walls give a unique minimum; uniqueness is not needed below. The initial squared radius relative to this selected minimum is E[X0^2], with no replacement by a later radius. A fixed weighted Euclidean isometry identifies this with an ordinary finite-dimensional instance.\n\nThe physical projection for a mean-zero X is\n\n    g_i=max(X_i-t,-a_i),   E g=0,\n\nwhere t>=0. Equivalently E(X-t+a)_+=a_bar. The actual update remains X'=X-hg. The wall labels a, all weights and the final body are fixed before the forward trajectory. A backward atom split duplicates the entire already-built future record, including its current wall label, before an explicitly justified modification.\n\n## 2. Exact inverse formulas, including the wall cross moment\n\nTake h=1+d>1, a mean-zero next law Y, and a real t satisfying\n\n    Z=t+d*a-Y>=0.\n\nChoose a right-branch probability omega_i in[0,1] with E[omega Z]=d*a_bar. The two inverse values are\n\n    X_L=Y-h*a=t-a-Z,\n    X_R=t-a+Z/d.\n\nTheir projection gradients are -a and -a+Z/d, respectively. Their weighted mean is0, as is their gradient mean, and both physical h updates give the same Y. Thus these are actual inverse points of one projection body. The mean-zero condition also forces t>=0.\n\nPut J=E[omega Z^2], J_a=E[omega a Z], and M_a=E[a^2]. Direct expansion gives\n\n    Var X=Var Y-2h E[aY]+h^2 M_a+2h*t*a_bar\n                         -(2h/d)J_a+(d^-2-1)J,\n    F_a(X)=t*a_bar+(d+1/2)M_a-E[aY]-(h/d)J_a+J/(2d^2),\n    ||P_(K_a)X||^2=M_a-(2/d)J_a+J/d^2.\n\nFor the value formula, write X-g=t on the upper branch and X-g=t-Z on the lower branch. Since E g=0, F_a(X)=E[g^2]/2+E[(1-omega)aZ]. This proves the displayed formula without a missing normal contribution.\n\nFor nonnegative LOCAL cost coefficients alpha,beta (not the benchmark sequences), define\n\n    C_(alpha,beta)(X)=Var X+2alpha F_a(X)+beta||P_(K_a)X||^2,\n    T=h+alpha, C0=1+alpha+beta,\n    K0=h^2+alpha(2d+1)+beta.\n\nThen the actual full cost is\n\n    C_(alpha,beta)(X)\n     =Var Y-2T E[aY]+K0 M_a+2T*t*a_bar\n       -[2(h(1+alpha)+beta)/d]J_a+(C0/d^2-1)J.       (1)\n\nThe wall cross moment is real data, not a free scalar reserve.\n\n## 3. The same primitive inverse-cost identity survives\n\nAssume C0>d^2. Define\n\n    w=C0/d^2-1>0, gamma=d+2T/w,\n    R=Y+gamma*a.\n\nChoose the unique z with E(R-z)_+=gamma*a_bar. Since E R=gamma*a_bar, one has z>=0. Select all atoms with R>z, optionally a fraction of boundary atoms with R=z, and none with R<z. Let I denote this possibly split selected set, p=P(I)>0, a_I=E[a*1_I]. Set\n\n    t=z+(d+gamma)(a_bar-a_I)/p.                    (2)\n\nThis t is nonnegative and gives E[1_I Z]=d*a_bar. It is an admissible inverse threshold if and only if the actual support guard\n\n    t>=max_i(Y_i-d*a_i)                            (3)\n\nholds. Unselected atoms already satisfy (3), since R_i<=z and a_i>=0. Selected atoms still need it. No physical child is rescaled in this construction.\n\nWrite U=(R-z)_+. The auxiliary SAME-body projection is\n\n    q=P_(K_a)(Y/gamma)=U/gamma-a.\n\nFrom w( gamma-d )=2T and direct substitution,\n\n    2(h(1+alpha)+beta)/d=w(d+gamma),\n    K0=w*gamma*d.\n\nTherefore the last two terms of (1) combine as w E[1_I Z(t-R)]. Using E U=gamma*a_bar gives\n\n    C_(alpha,beta)(X)\n     =Var Y-2T E[aY]+K0 M_a+2T*z*a_bar\n                        -w(d+gamma)E[aU]+w E[U^2].             (4)\n\nAlso, by expanding the actual q,\n\n    U_gamma(Y):=2gamma F_a(Y/gamma)\n       =2z*a_bar+E[U^2]/gamma-2E[aY]-gamma*M_a,\n    ||q||^2=E[U^2]/gamma^2-2E[aU]/gamma+M_a.\n\nConsequently (4) is exactly\n\n    C_(alpha,beta)(X)=Var Y+T U_gamma(Y)+B_gamma||q||^2,\n    B_gamma=w gamma^2-T gamma=gamma(wd+T)>0.         (5)\n\nThus the guarded identity from R9 is not limited to equal lower walls. In particular alpha=C_L-1, beta=C_L(C_L-1) and d(C_L+C_R+d)=C_L*C_R yield gamma=C_R and B_gamma=(C_L+d)(C_L+C_R+d), exactly as in the R9 parent note. This remains a conditional attained identity, not a closed composition theorem.\n\n## 4. A genuine future-preserving repair of the support guard\n\nSuppose an entire finite future physical trajectory has already been built with this body, starting at Y and including every future terminal evaluation. Suppose EACH selected atom i in I is unsaturated at every such query: its recorded gradient satisfies\n\n    g_i^(k)=X_i^(k)-t_k\n\nfor every future query k. Equality at the clipping boundary is allowed; a strictly clipped lower branch is not.\n\nIncrease a_i, only on selected atoms, to any larger finite values. Each old unsaturated projection formula remains unchanged at every future query: max(X_i^(k)-t_k,-a_i) still equals the recorded g_i^(k). Other atoms and all means remain unchanged. Therefore every recorded t_k still solves the projection equation, all future actual gradients/iterates and objective values stay EXACTLY the same. The terminal objective is preserved, and0 remains a legal selected minimizer.\n\nThe auxiliary projection q in Section3 is also unchanged. On selected atoms U_i increases by gamma times the wall increase; both E U and gamma*a_bar increase equally. Thus z, selected massp and the set I can be kept fixed (a selected boundary atom may become strictly selected). Because a_bar-a_I is unchanged, (2) keeps the SAME t. On each increased atom Y_i-d*a_i decreases, while unselected atoms already satisfy (3). Choose\n\n    new a_i>=max(old a_i,(Y_i-t)/d)\n\non the selected atoms. This makes the support guard hold everywhere. Formula (5) then constructs an actual predecessor, at the SAME cost on its right-hand side: VarY, q and U_gamma(Y) did not change.\n\nAll choices can be made backwards before the actual forward run. Atom duplication preserves its whole recorded future, and the change above is justified by every recorded query, not only its endpoint. The terminal function is one fixed K_a after all changes; there is no function change during execution.\n\nThis is a zero-additional-cost guard repair under an explicit persistent-unsaturation condition. It is NOT permission to increase the wall of an atom that was strictly saturated at any already-built future query. At such a query the old gradient generally changes. Nor does it prove that the selected upper-tail atoms at the next inverse step enjoy the condition.\n\nThe hypothesis can be weakened to just the selected atoms that actually require an increase: those with Y_i-d*a_i>t. All other selected walls can be left fixed. Such a violating atom necessarily has Y_i>0, since t>=0 and a_i>=0. This observation narrows the required persistent-unsaturation property; it does not prove it after a reflected inverse branch.\n\nIf an already constructed proof also relies on other auxiliary evaluations, their projection records must be included among the samples preserved by this operation, or their changed contributions must be paid separately. Preserving the physical future alone preserves its objective and chronology, but does not automatically preserve a previous auxiliary-potential bookkeeping argument.\n\n## 5. The actual remaining composition requirement\n\nAn all-word or full-H_m construction must preserve enough persistent-unsaturated atoms to perform Section4 at every needed inverse join, while controlling the entire prefix cost and terminal scale. The current note does not prove that invariant, nor give an original variance bound at the benchmark scale. It does identify an actual operation that repairs the previous real guard while preserving all built future data; it is stronger than renaming that guard.\n\nAny attempt using this operation must show, for every selected atom and every later physical query, why no strict lower saturation occurred. A high terminal value alone does not imply that history property. If the needed selected atom has already saturated, the free repair is unavailable and the cost of any alternative must be paid explicitly.\n\nNo original minimax conclusion, full-class upper bound, formal acceptance, or permission to renew old grants follows. The note is an UNVERIFIED lead for a fresh bounded direct construction, and all original target quantifiers remain unchanged.\n", "verifiable": false, "status": "open", "fact_id": null, "links": {"preceding_elaboration": "5bac6182b84623b8", "parent_note_sha256": "8a77974afa9995b0758ec77c96305de0d732dc1722680339c4823c9a8d725040", "original_target": "OPEN", "goal_status": "active", "new_formal_allowance": 0}, "glossary": {}}

The draft constructs that invariant exactly and decomposes its entire nested-face trajectory into charged one-release modes and an affine mode. Its component is the exact single-cut term in `b98bb807d514e80d`; an orthogonal positive sum produces a radius-weighted average. A different original minimum must be valid for the final fixed body, and its original distance must be proved; the selected0 ceiling does not automatically transfer.\n\n“Paying the input and right transport normals closes the upper induction.” Status: DANGEROUS. The draft now pays those normals using actual samples. Yet the exact remainder is negative on legal interval data if both child surpluses are discarded. The actual invariant is positive there, so this is failure of the payment rule, not the proposition.\n\n“Positive interval modes provide a new way to retune the diagonal certificate.” Status: FALSE AS STATED for the displayed dictionary. The central-cut obstruction extends to every finite positive sum of A/D atoms, with arbitrary positive within-interval weights and nonnegative physical diagonal slack. Non-diagonal remainders or different function weights are outside that result, but need a real certificate.\n\n“A positive Brownian-bridge covariance proves the dominance residual positive.” Status: DANGEROUS. Only one summand is automatically PSD.
